#### Example 3

English: This place feels like home.

Roman Nepali: Yo thau aafno ghar jasto lagchha.

Meaning: Comfort and belonging.

### Memory Trick

**Home = Feeling and living place**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| House | Building | Physical structure |
| Home | Aafno ghar | Living place/feeling |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. We bought a new ______.
2. I am going ______ now.
3. She stayed at ______ all day.

Answers:

1. house
2. home
3. home

---

## Quick Revision

* House = Building
* Home = Living place

---

# 123. End / Finish

## Type

Confusing Verb

---

## Word 1: End

### Simple English Meaning

To stop or reach the final point.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Antya hunu

### Use

Events, periods, stories.

### Where to Use

✅ End a meeting

✅ End a story

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Completing a task personally (often finish is better)

### Structure (if applicable)

End + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ I ended my homework.

✅ I finished my homework.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The movie ended at 9 PM.

Roman Nepali: Film 9 baje sakiyo.

Meaning: Reached final point.

#### Example 2

English: The meeting ended early.

Roman Nepali: Baithak chadai sakiyo.

Meaning: Came to an end.

#### Example 3

English: The war ended.

Roman Nepali: Yuddha samapta bhayo.

Meaning: Finished completely.

### Memory Trick

**End = Final point**

---

## Word 2: Finish

### Simple English Meaning

To complete something.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Pura garnu

### Use

Tasks, work, homework.

### Where to Use

✅ Finish homework

✅ Finish work

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Large events ending naturally

### Structure (if applicable)

Finish + task

### Common Mistake

❌ The concert finished itself. (possible, but ended is often preferred)

✅ The concert ended.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I finished my homework.

Roman Nepali: Maile grihakarya pura gare.

Meaning: Completed task.

#### Example 2

English: She finished the project.

Roman Nepali: Unile project pura garin.

Meaning: Completed work.

#### Example 3

English: We finished eating.

Roman Nepali: Hami le khana sakyau.

Meaning: Completed action.

### Memory Trick

**Finish = Complete a task**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| End | Antya hunu | Events, periods |
| Finish | Pura garnu | Tasks, work |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. I ______ my homework.
2. The meeting ______ at noon.
3. She ______ the project yesterday.

Answers:

1. finished
2. ended
3. finished

---

## Quick Revision

* End = Reach final point
* Finish = Complete task

---

# 124. Last / Latest

## Type

Look-Alike Word

---

## Word 1: Last

### Simple English Meaning

Final or previous.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Antim / Pahilo paltko

### Use

Previous time or final position.

### Where to Use

✅ Last week

✅ Last person

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Most recent version/news

### Structure (if applicable)

Last + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ What is the last news?

✅ What is the latest news?

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I saw him last week.

Roman Nepali: Maile unilai gata hapta dekhe.

Meaning: Previous week.

#### Example 2

English: She came last in the race.

Roman Nepali: Uni daudma antim bhain.

Meaning: Final position.

#### Example 3

English: This is my last chance.

Roman Nepali: Yo mero antim awasar ho.

Meaning: Final opportunity.

### Memory Trick

**Last = Previous or final**

---

## Word 2: Latest

### Simple English Meaning

Most recent.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Sabai bhanda naya

### Use

News, updates, versions.

### Where to Use

✅ Latest news

✅ Latest model

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Final person in a line

### Structure (if applicable)

Latest + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ He was the latest runner.

✅ He was the last runner.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: What is the latest news?

Roman Nepali: Sabai bhanda naya samachar ke ho?

Meaning: Most recent information.

#### Example 2

English: I bought the latest phone.

Roman Nepali: Maile sabai bhanda naya phone kine.

Meaning: Newest version.

#### Example 3

English: She read the latest report.

Roman Nepali: Unile naya pratibedan padhin.

Meaning: Most recent document.

### Memory Trick

**Latest = Newest**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Last | Previous/final | Time or position |
| Latest | Newest | Recent information |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. What is the ______ news?
2. I met him ______ month.
3. She bought the ______ model.

Answers:

2. last
3. latest

---

## Quick Revision

* Last = Previous/final
* Latest = Newest

# 125. Elder / Older

## Type

Look-Alike Word

---

## Word 1: Elder

### Simple English Meaning

Older person in a family.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Jetho

### Use

Mostly family relationships.

### Where to Use

✅ Elder brother

✅ Elder sister

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Most objects and general comparisons

### Structure (if applicable)

Elder + family member

### Common Mistake

❌ My elder car is red.

✅ My older car is red.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: My elder brother lives abroad.

Roman Nepali: Mero jetho dai bideshma basnuhunchha.

Meaning: Older family member.

#### Example 2

English: She is my elder sister.

Roman Nepali: Uni meri jethi didi hun.

Meaning: Older sibling.

#### Example 3

English: Respect your elders.

Roman Nepali: Aafno jethabada ko samman gara.

Meaning: Older people.

### Memory Trick

**Elder = Family**

---

## Word 2: Older

### Simple English Meaning

Having more age.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Badi umar ko

### Use

General comparisons.

### Where to Use

✅ Older man

✅ Older building

✅ Older car

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Fixed family titles (often elder is preferred)

### Structure (if applicable)

Older than + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ My older brother. (Correct, but elder brother is more traditional.)

✅ My elder brother.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: He is older than me.

Roman Nepali: Uni bhanda ma sano chu.

Meaning: Greater age.

#### Example 2

English: This building is older.

Roman Nepali: Yo building purano cha.

Meaning: More age.

#### Example 3

English: She married an older man.

Roman Nepali: Unile aafu bhanda badi umar ko manchhesanga bihe garin.

Meaning: More age.

### Memory Trick

**Older = General age comparison**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Elder | Jetho | Family relationships |
| Older | Badi umar ko | General comparison |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. My ______ brother is a doctor.
2. He is ______ than me.
3. Respect your ______.

Answers:

1. elder
2. older
3. elders

---

## Quick Revision

* Elder = Family
* Older = General age

-------
# 126. Farther / Further

## Type

Look-Alike Word

---

## Word 1: Farther

### Simple English Meaning

At a greater physical distance.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Ajhai tada

### Use

Physical distance only.

### Where to Use

✅ Farther down the road

✅ Farther from school

✅ Farther away

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Ideas, discussions, education, progress

### Structure (if applicable)

Farther than + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ We need farther information.

✅ We need further information.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Pokhara is farther than Dhulikhel.

Roman Nepali: Pokhara Dhulikhel bhanda tada cha.

Meaning: Greater distance.

#### Example 2

English: Walk farther down the street.

Roman Nepali: Sadakma ajhai tada hid.

Meaning: More distance.

#### Example 3

English: The shop is farther away.

Roman Nepali: Pasal ajhai tada cha.

Meaning: Physical distance.

### Memory Trick

**Farther = Distance**

---

## Word 2: Further

### Simple English Meaning

More, additional, or deeper.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Ajhai thap

### Use

Information, discussion, study, progress.

### Where to Use

✅ Further information

✅ Further study

✅ Further discussion

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Physical distance (traditional grammar)

### Structure (if applicable)

Further + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ I need farther details.

✅ I need further details.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: We need further information.

Roman Nepali: Hamilai ajhai thap jankari chahinchha.

Meaning: Additional information.

#### Example 2

English: Further discussion is needed.

Roman Nepali: Ajhai thap chhalphal awasyak cha.

Meaning: More discussion.

#### Example 3

English: She continued her further studies.

Roman Nepali: Unile ajhai thap padhai jari rakhin.

Meaning: Advanced education.

### Memory Trick

**Further = More**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Farther | Ajhai tada | Physical distance |
| Further | Ajhai thap | Additional/progress |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. We need ______ information.
2. The village is ______ away.
3. No ______ discussion is required.

Answers:

1. further
2. farther
3. further

---

## Quick Revision

* Farther = Distance
* Further = More

---

# 127. Beside / Besides

## Type

Look-Alike Word

---

## Word 1: Beside

### Simple English Meaning

Next to.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Chheu ma

### Use

Position or location.

### Where to Use

✅ Beside the table

✅ Beside me

✅ Beside the road

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Meaning "in addition to"

### Structure (if applicable)

Beside + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Beside English, I know Nepali.

✅ Besides English, I know Nepali.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Sit beside me.

Roman Nepali: Mero chheu ma basa.

Meaning: Next to me.

#### Example 2

English: The school is beside the bank.

Roman Nepali: School bankko chheu ma cha.

Meaning: Nearby position.

#### Example 3

English: She stood beside her friend.

Roman Nepali: Uni sathiko chheu ma ubhin.

Meaning: Next to someone.

### Memory Trick

**Beside = Side by side**

---

## Word 2: Besides

### Simple English Meaning

In addition to.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Bahek / Thap

### Use

Adding information.

### Where to Use

✅ Besides English

✅ Besides this

✅ Besides that

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Physical location

### Structure (if applicable)

Besides + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ The chair is besides the table.

✅ The chair is beside the table.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Besides English, she speaks Hindi.

Roman Nepali: English bahek uni Hindi pani bolchhin.

Meaning: In addition.

#### Example 2

English: Besides this, we need money.

Roman Nepali: Yes bahek hamilai paisa pani chahinchha.

Meaning: Additional thing.

#### Example 3

English: Besides Ram, Hari came too.

Roman Nepali: Ram bahek Hari pani aayo.

Meaning: Also.

### Memory Trick

**Besides = Plus**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Beside | Chheu ma | Position |
| Besides | Bahek / Plus | Addition |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. Sit ______ me.
2. ______ English, she knows French.
3. The shop is ______ the hospital.

Answers:

1. beside
2. Besides
3. beside

---

## Quick Revision

* Beside = Next to
* Besides = In addition

---

# 128. Across / Cross

## Type

Confusing Word

---

## Word 1: Across

### Simple English Meaning

From one side to the other side.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Pari tira

### Use

Shows position or movement.

### Where to Use

✅ Across the river

✅ Across the road

### Where NOT to Use

❌ As a main action verb

### Structure (if applicable)

Across + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ I across the road.

✅ I cross the road.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The bank is across the road.

Roman Nepali: Bank sadakko pari tira cha.

Meaning: Opposite side.

#### Example 2

English: He swam across the river.

Roman Nepali: Uni nadiko pari tir samma tairiye.

Meaning: From one side to another.

#### Example 3

English: We looked across the valley.

Roman Nepali: Hami upatyakako pari tira heriyau.

Meaning: Opposite direction.

### Memory Trick

**Across = Position or direction**

---

## Word 2: Cross

### Simple English Meaning

To go from one side to another.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Par garnu

### Use

Action.

### Where to Use

✅ Cross the road

✅ Cross the bridge

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Showing location

### Structure (if applicable)

Cross + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ The bank is cross the road.

✅ The bank is across the road.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Cross the road carefully.

Roman Nepali: Sadak dhyan diyera par gara.

Meaning: Move across.

#### Example 2

English: We crossed the bridge.

Roman Nepali: Hami pul par garyau.

Meaning: Went to the other side.

#### Example 3

English: She crossed the river.

Roman Nepali: Unile nadi pari garin.

Meaning: Moved across.

### Memory Trick

**Cross = Action**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Across | Pari tira | Position/direction |
| Cross | Par garnu | Action |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The school is ______ the street.
2. Please ______ the road carefully.
3. We swam ______ the river.

Answers:

1. across
2. cross
3. across

---

## Quick Revision

* Across = Position
* Cross = Action

---

# 129. During / While

## Type

Confusing Word

---

## Word 1: During

### Simple English Meaning

At some time in a period.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Ko bela ma

### Use

Before a noun.

### Where to Use

✅ During class

✅ During lunch

✅ During vacation

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Before a full sentence

### Structure (if applicable)

During + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ During I was sleeping.

✅ While I was sleeping.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Do not talk during class.

Roman Nepali: Kaksha ko bela ma nabola.

Meaning: In that period.

#### Example 2

English: It rained during the match.

Roman Nepali: Matchko bela ma pani paryo.

Meaning: Within that time.

#### Example 3

English: He slept during the movie.

Roman Nepali: Filmko bela ma uni sutey.

Meaning: In that period.

### Memory Trick

**During + Noun**

---

## Word 2: While

### Simple English Meaning

At the same time that.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Jaba samma

### Use

Before a clause.

### Where to Use

✅ While I was studying

✅ While she was cooking

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Before only a noun

### Structure (if applicable)

While + subject + verb

### Common Mistake

❌ While the meeting.

✅ During the meeting.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: While I was studying, he called.

Roman Nepali: Ma padhdai thie jaba usle phone garyo.

Meaning: Same time.

#### Example 2

English: She listened to music while working.

Roman Nepali: Uni kaam garda sangeet sundai thiin.

Meaning: Two actions together.

#### Example 3

English: While we were eating, it rained.

Roman Nepali: Hami khadai thiyau jaba pani paryo.

Meaning: Simultaneous actions.

### Memory Trick

**While + Sentence**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| During | Ko bela ma | Before noun |
| While | Jaba samma | Before clause |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. Do not talk ______ class.
2. ______ I was sleeping, the phone rang.
3. It rained ______ the game.

Answers:

1. during
2. While
3. during

---

## Quick Revision

* During + Noun
* While + Sentence

---

# 130. Among / Between

## Type

Confusing Preposition

---

## Word 1: Among

### Simple English Meaning

In the middle of three or more people or things.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Bichma

### Use

Three or more.

### Where to Use

✅ Among friends

✅ Among students

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Only two people/things

### Structure (if applicable)

Among + plural noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Divide the cake among two children.

✅ Divide the cake between two children.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: She sat among her friends.

Roman Nepali: Uni sathiharuko bichma basiin.

Meaning: Surrounded by many.

#### Example 2

English: The secret spread among students.

Roman Nepali: Rahasya bidyarthiharuma phailiyo.

Meaning: Many people.

#### Example 3

English: He is popular among teachers.

Roman Nepali: Uni shikshakharubich lokpriya chan.

Meaning: Group of people.

### Memory Trick

**Among = Many**

---

## Word 2: Between

### Simple English Meaning

In the middle of two people, places, or things.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Dui ko bichma

### Use

Two things.

### Where to Use

✅ Between Ram and Hari

✅ Between two buildings

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Large group generally

### Structure (if applicable)

Between A and B

### Common Mistake

❌ Between all students.

✅ Among all students.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The ball is between the chairs.

Roman Nepali: Ball dui kursiko bichma cha.

Meaning: Middle of two things.

#### Example 2

English: Choose between tea and coffee.

Roman Nepali: Chiya ra coffee bich chhanot gara.

Meaning: Two options.

#### Example 3

English: The discussion was between Ram and Hari.

Roman Nepali: Chhalphal Ram ra Hari bich thiyo.

Meaning: Two people.

### Memory Trick

**Between = Two**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Among | Bichma | Three or more |
| Between | Dui ko bichma | Two |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The school is ______ two banks.
2. She is popular ______ students.
3. Divide the money ______ Ram and Hari.

Answers:

1. between
2. among
3. between

---

## Quick Revision

* Among = Three or more
* Between = Two

-------
# 131. Each / Every

## Type

Confusing Word

---

## Word 1: Each

### Simple English Meaning

Every person or thing separately.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Pratyek

### Use

When thinking about individuals one by one.

### Where to Use

✅ Each student

✅ Each book

✅ Each person

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Large groups when individual focus is not important

### Structure (if applicable)

Each + singular noun

Each of + plural noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Each students received a certificate.

✅ Each student received a certificate.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Each student received a book.

Roman Nepali: Pratyek bidyarthile kitab payo.

Meaning: One by one.

#### Example 2

English: Each of the players was ready.

Roman Nepali: Pratyek kheladi tayar thiyo.

Meaning: Individual focus.

#### Example 3

English: I spoke to each person.

Roman Nepali: Maile pratyek byaktisanga kura gare.

Meaning: One at a time.

### Memory Trick

**Each = One by one**

---

## Word 2: Every

### Simple English Meaning

All people or things in a group.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Harek

### Use

Whole group together.

### Where to Use

✅ Every day

✅ Every student

✅ Every house

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Directly before "of"

### Structure (if applicable)

Every + singular noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Every of the students came.

✅ Every student came.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Every student passed the test.

Roman Nepali: Harek bidyarthi pass bhayo.

Meaning: Entire group.

#### Example 2

English: I exercise every day.

Roman Nepali: Ma harek din byayam garchu.

Meaning: Daily.

#### Example 3

English: Every room was clean.

Roman Nepali: Harek kotha safa thiyo.

Meaning: All rooms.

### Memory Trick

**Every = All in a group**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Each | Pratyek | Individual focus |
| Every | Harek | Whole group |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. ______ student got a pen.
2. I walk ______ morning.
3. I checked ______ answer carefully.

Answers:

1. Every
2. every
3. each

---

## Quick Revision

* Each = One by one
* Every = Whole group

---

# 132. Few / A Few / Little / A Little

## Type

Confusing Quantifiers

---

## Word 1: Few

### Simple English Meaning

Not many; almost none.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Dherai chaina

### Use

Countable nouns.

### Where to Use

✅ Few students

✅ Few books

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Uncountable nouns

### Structure (if applicable)

Few + plural noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Few water remained.

✅ Little water remained.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Few students attended.

Roman Nepali: Thorai bidyarthi aaye.

Meaning: Almost none.

#### Example 2

English: Few people agreed.

Roman Nepali: Thorai manchhe sahamat bhaye.

Meaning: Small number.

#### Example 3

English: Few books were available.

Roman Nepali: Thorai kitab upalabdha thiye.

Meaning: Not many.

### Memory Trick

**Few = Almost not enough**

---

## Word 2: A Few

### Simple English Meaning

Some; enough.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Kehi

### Use

Countable nouns.

### Where to Use

✅ A few friends

✅ A few questions

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Uncountable nouns

### Structure (if applicable)

A few + plural noun

### Common Mistake

❌ A few milk is left.

✅ A little milk is left.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I have a few friends here.

Roman Nepali: Mera yaha kehi sathi chan.

Meaning: Some friends.

#### Example 2

English: She asked a few questions.

Roman Nepali: Unile kehi prasna sodhin.

Meaning: Some questions.

#### Example 3

English: We need a few chairs.

Roman Nepali: Hamilai kehi kursi chahinchha.

Meaning: Small but enough number.

### Memory Trick

**A few = Some and enough**

---

## Word 3: Little

### Simple English Meaning

Not much; almost none.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Dherai chaina

### Use

Uncountable nouns.

### Where to Use

✅ Little water

✅ Little money

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Countable nouns

### Structure (if applicable)

Little + uncountable noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Little books remained.

✅ Few books remained.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: There is little water left.

Roman Nepali: Thorai pani matra baki cha.

Meaning: Almost none.

#### Example 2

English: He has little money.

Roman Nepali: Unisanga thorai paisa cha.

Meaning: Not much.

#### Example 3

English: We have little time.

Roman Nepali: Hamisanga thorai samay cha.

Meaning: Almost insufficient.

### Memory Trick

**Little = Almost not enough (uncountable)**

---

## Word 4: A Little

### Simple English Meaning

Some; enough.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Ali ali

### Use

Uncountable nouns.

### Where to Use

✅ A little water

✅ A little money

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Countable nouns

### Structure (if applicable)

A little + uncountable noun

### Common Mistake

❌ A little books.

✅ A few books.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I need a little help.

Roman Nepali: Malai ali ali sahayog chahinchha.

Meaning: Some help.

#### Example 2

English: She drank a little water.

Roman Nepali: Unile ali ali pani piin.

Meaning: Small amount.

#### Example 3

English: We have a little time.

Roman Nepali: Hamisanga ali ali samay cha.

Meaning: Some time available.

### Memory Trick

**A little = Some and enough**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Few | Almost none | Countable |
| A Few | Some | Countable |
| Little | Almost none | Uncountable |
| A Little | Some | Uncountable |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. We have ______ water left.
2. ______ students attended the class.
3. I have ______ friends here.

Answers:

1. little
2. Few
3. a few

---

## Quick Revision

* Few = Not many
* A Few = Some
* Little = Not much
* A Little = Some

---

# 133. Much / Many

## Type

Confusing Quantifier

---

## Word 1: Much

### Simple English Meaning

A large amount.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Dherai

### Use

Uncountable nouns.

### Where to Use

✅ Much water

✅ Much time

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Countable plural nouns

### Structure (if applicable)

Much + uncountable noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Much books.

✅ Many books.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I don't have much money.

Roman Nepali: Masanga dherai paisa chaina.

Meaning: Amount.

#### Example 2

English: How much water do you need?

Roman Nepali: Timilai kati pani chahinchha?

Meaning: Quantity.

#### Example 3

English: There isn't much time.

Roman Nepali: Dherai samay chaina.

Meaning: Amount of time.

### Memory Trick

**Much = Uncountable**

---

## Word 2: Many

### Simple English Meaning

A large number.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Dherai

### Use

Countable nouns.

### Where to Use

✅ Many books

✅ Many students

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Uncountable nouns

### Structure (if applicable)

Many + plural noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Many water.

✅ Much water.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Many students came.

Roman Nepali: Dherai bidyarthi aaye.

Meaning: Large number.

#### Example 2

English: We have many friends.

Roman Nepali: Hamisanga dherai sathi chan.

Meaning: Number of people.

#### Example 3

English: Many cars were parked outside.

Roman Nepali: Bahira dherai gaadi park gareka thiye.

Meaning: Large count.

### Memory Trick

**Many = Countable**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Much | Dherai | Uncountable |
| Many | Dherai | Countable |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. How ______ money do you have?
2. ______ students attended.
3. We don't have ______ time.

Answers:

1. much
2. Many
3. much

---

## Quick Revision

* Much = Uncountable
* Many = Countable

Look-Alike Words

### Common Mistake

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: It was a historic moment.

Meaning: Important in history.

#### Example 2

English: The team achieved a historic win.

Meaning: Memorable achievement.

#### Example 3

English: We visited a historic site.

Confusing Words

## Word 1: Assure

### Use

### Where to Use

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Making something certain

### Structure (if applicable)

### Common Mistake

❌ I assured success.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I assure you everything is fine.

Roman Nepali: Ma timilai biswas dilauchhu sabai thik cha.

#### Example 2

English: She assured her parents.

Meaning: Reassured people.

#### Example 3

English: He assured me that he would come.

Roman Nepali: Unile malai aaune biswas dilaye.

Meaning: Promised confidently.

### Memory Trick

---

## Word 2: Ensure

### Simple English Meaning

To make certain that something happens.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

### Use

### Where to Use

✅ Ensure safety

### Where NOT to Use

Ensure that + clause

### Common Mistake

## Word 3: Insure

Bima garnu

### Use

Insurance policies.

### Where to Use

### Where NOT to Use

### Structure (if applicable)

### Common Mistake

Meaning: Bought insurance.

#### Example 2

English: They insured the building.

Roman Nepali: Uniharule buildingko bima gare.

Meaning: Financial protection.

#### Example 3

English: The company insures homes.

Roman Nepali: Companyle gharharuko bima garchha.

Meaning: Provides insurance.

### Memory Trick

**Insure = Insurance**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|--------|---------|---------|
| Assure | Reassure | Person |
| Ensure | Make certain | Result |
| Insure | Provide insurance | Property |

---

## Exam Pattern

1. I ______ you that everything is okay.
2. We must ______ safety.
3. He decided to ______ his car.

2. ensure
3. insure

---

## Quick Revision

* Assure = Person
* Ensure = Result
* Insure = Insurance

Look-Alike Words

* Allusion = Reference
* Illusion = Bhram

## Word 1: Emigrate

### Simple English Meaning

To leave your country and move to another.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Desh chhodnu

### Use

Leaving a country.

### Where to Use

✅ Emigrate from Nepal

✅ Emigrate from India

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Arriving in a new country

### Structure (if applicable)

Emigrate from + country

### Common Mistake

❌ He emigrated to Canada from Nepal. (possible, but focus is leaving Nepal)

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: They emigrated from Nepal.

Roman Nepali: Uniharu Nepal chhode.

Meaning: Left their country.

#### Example 2

English: Many people emigrated during that period.

Roman Nepali: Dherai manchhe tyo samayma desh chhode.

Meaning: Moved away.

#### Example 3

English: Her family emigrated years ago.

Roman Nepali: Unko parivar barsau agadi desh chhodyo.

Meaning: Left homeland.

### Memory Trick

**Emigrate = Exit country**

---

## Word 2: Immigrate

### Simple English Meaning

To come into a new country to live.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Naya deshma basna aaunu

### Use

Entering a country.

### Where to Use

✅ Immigrate to Canada

✅ Immigrate to Australia

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Leaving a country

### Structure (if applicable)

Immigrate to + country

### Common Mistake

❌ They immigrated from Nepal. (focus should be destination)

✅ They immigrated to Canada.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: They immigrated to Canada.

Roman Nepali: Uniharu Canada basna gaye.

Meaning: Entered new country.

#### Example 2

English: Many families immigrate every year.

Roman Nepali: Dherai parivar harek barsa naya deshma janchhan.

Meaning: Move into a country.

#### Example 3

English: She immigrated to Australia.

Roman Nepali: Uni Australia basna gain.

Meaning: Arrived to live there.

### Memory Trick

**Immigrate = Into country**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Emigrate | Leave country | From |
| Immigrate | Enter country | To |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. They ______ from Nepal.
2. She ______ to Australia.
3. Many families ______ to Canada each year.

Answers:

1. emigrated
2. immigrated
3. immigrate

---

## Quick Revision

* Emigrate = Leave
* Immigrate = Enter

---

Look-Alike Words

✅ An imminent danger is near.

#### Example 2

Meaning: Well known expert.

#### Example 3

English: He became an eminent writer.

Meaning: Famous person.

### Where NOT to Use

#### Example 3

English: The announcement is imminent.

**Imminent = Immediate future**

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Eminent | Famous and respected | People |
| Imminent | About to happen | Events |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. He is an ______ professor.
2. The danger is ______.
3. She is an ______ researcher.

2. imminent
3. eminent

---

## Quick Revision

* Eminent = Famous person
* Imminent = Happening soon

Look-Alike Words

### Where NOT to Use

### Structure (if applicable)

### Common Mistake

#### Example 1

English: He is respectful to his teachers.

Meaning: Shows respect.

English: She gave a respectful reply.

Meaning: Polite response.

#### Example 3

English: Respectful behavior is important.

## Word 2: Respective

Belonging separately to each person or thing.

### Use

Groups, ownership, assignments.

### Where NOT to Use

Meaning: Individual groups.

#### Example 3

English: Please contact your respective manager.

Meaning: The correct person for each group.

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|

| Respective | Aafno aafno | Separate ownership |

## Exam Pattern

3. Players joined their ______ teams.

2. respectful
3. respective

---

## Quick Revision

* Respectful = Polite
* Respective = Aafno aafno

# 150. Continual / Continuous

## Type

Look-Alike Words

---

## Word 1: Continual

### Simple English Meaning

Happening again and again with breaks.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Barambar

### Use

Repeated actions.

### Where to Use

✅ Continual interruptions

✅ Continual complaints

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Without stopping

### Structure (if applicable)

Continual + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ The river flows continual.

✅ The river flows continuously.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: We faced continual delays.

Roman Nepali: Hami le barambar dhilaiko samna garyau.

Meaning: Repeated many times.

#### Example 2

English: His continual questions annoyed everyone.

Roman Nepali: Unka barambar prasnale sabailai dikka paryo.

Meaning: Repeated often.

#### Example 3

English: There were continual interruptions.

Roman Nepali: Barambar badha aairahe.

Meaning: Again and again.

### Memory Trick

**Continual = Repeated**

---

## Word 2: Continuous

### Simple English Meaning

Without stopping.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Lagatar

### Use

Ongoing action with no break.

### Where to Use

✅ Continuous rain

✅ Continuous noise

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Repeated actions with pauses

### Structure (if applicable)

Continuous + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Continuous phone calls all day. (if there were breaks)

✅ Continual phone calls all day.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: It rained continuously for two days.

Roman Nepali: Dui din samma lagatar pani paryo.

Meaning: No stop.

#### Example 2

English: The machine runs continuously.

Roman Nepali: Machine lagatar chalchha.

Meaning: Without interruption.

#### Example 3

English: We heard continuous noise.

Roman Nepali: Hami le lagatar awaaj sunyau.

Meaning: Never stopped.

### Memory Trick

**Continuous = No break**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Continual | Repeated with breaks | Again and again |
| Continuous | Without stopping | No break |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The machine ran ______ for 24 hours.
2. We experienced ______ interruptions.
3. The rain was ______ all night.

Answers:

1. continuously
2. continual
3. continuous

---

## Quick Revision

* Continual = Repeated
* Continuous = No break

British English.

✅ Dual citizenship

### Where NOT to Use

❌ A fight between two people

### Structure (if applicable)

Dual + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ The knights fought a dual.

✅ The knights fought a duel.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: She has dual citizenship.

Roman Nepali: Unisanga dui deshko nagarikta cha.

Meaning: Two citizenships.

#### Example 2

English: This phone has dual cameras.

Roman Nepali: Yo phonema dui camera chan.

Meaning: Two parts.

#### Example 3

English: It serves a dual purpose.

Roman Nepali: Yesko dui uddeshya chan.

Meaning: Two functions.

### Memory Trick

**Dual = Two**

---

## Word 2: Duel

### Simple English Meaning

A fight or contest between two people.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Dui jana bichko mukabala

### Use

Competition, combat.

### Where to Use

✅ Sword duel

✅ Duel between rivals

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Two functions or purposes

### Structure (if applicable)

Duel between + people

### Common Mistake

❌ This machine has a duel function.

✅ This machine has a dual function.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The two knights fought a duel.

Roman Nepali: Dui yoddhale mukabala gare.

Meaning: Fight between two.

#### Example 2

English: The duel lasted one hour.

Roman Nepali: Mukabala ek ghanta samma chalyo.

Meaning: Contest.

#### Example 3

English: The movie showed a dramatic duel.

Roman Nepali: Filmma romanchak mukabala dekhaio.

Meaning: Face-to-face fight.

### Memory Trick

**Duel = Fight between two**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Dual | Two parts | Functions, systems |
| Duel | Contest between two | Fight, competition |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. She has ______ citizenship.
2. The warriors fought a ______.
3. This machine serves a ______ purpose.

Answers:

1. dual
2. duel
3. dual

---

## Quick Revision

* Dual = Two parts
* Duel = Two-person fight

------

✅ Loath to admit

✅ Loath to leave

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Strongly hate something

### Structure (if applicable)

Loath + to + verb

### Common Mistake

❌ I loath doing homework.

✅ I loathe doing homework.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: He was loath to admit his mistake.

Roman Nepali: Uni aafno galti swikar garna ichchhuk thiyenan.

Meaning: Unwilling to do it.

#### Example 2

English: She is loath to leave her hometown.

Roman Nepali: Uni aafno janmabhumi chhodna manparaudinan.

Meaning: Reluctant.

#### Example 3

English: They were loath to change the plan.

Roman Nepali: Uniharu yojana badalna ichchhuk thienan.

Meaning: Did not want to do it.

### Memory Trick

**Loath = Reluctant**

---

## Word 2: Loathe

### Simple English Meaning

To hate something very much.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Ghrina garnu

### Use

Strong dislike.

### Where to Use

✅ Loathe dishonesty

✅ Loathe violence

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Unwillingness only

### Structure (if applicable)

Loathe + noun/verb

### Common Mistake

❌ She was loathe to mathematics.

✅ She loathes mathematics.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I loathe dishonesty.

Roman Nepali: Ma jhutho kurako ghrina garchhu.

Meaning: Hate strongly.

#### Example 2

English: He loathes waking up early.

Roman Nepali: Uni chadai uthna manparaudainan.

Meaning: Strong dislike.

#### Example 3

English: They loathe corruption.

Roman Nepali: Uniharu bhrastachar ko ghrina garchhan.

Meaning: Hate very much.

### Memory Trick

**Loathe = Hate**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Loath | Reluctant | Unwilling to do |
| Loathe | Hate | Strong dislike |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. She ______ cheating.
2. He was ______ to speak.
3. I ______ waiting in long lines.

Answers:

1. loathes
2. loath
3. loathe

---

## Quick Revision

* Loath = Unwilling
* Loathe = Hate

---

### Use

### Where NOT to Use

### Structure (if applicable)

#### Example 3

# 172. Precede / Proceed

Look-Alike Words

## Word 1: Precede

### Use

### Where NOT to Use

### Structure (if applicable)

### Common Mistake

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: A introduction preceded the ceremony.

#### Example 3

English: The speech preceded the award ceremony.

### Use

# 175. Respectfully / Respectively

## Type

Look-Alike Words

---

## Word 1: Respectfully

### Simple English Meaning

In a polite and respectful way.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Samman purbak

### Use

Speaking, writing, requests.

### Where to Use

✅ Respectfully ask

✅ Respectfully disagree

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Matching items in order

### Structure (if applicable)

Respectfully + verb

### Common Mistake

❌ Ram and Hari scored 70 and 80 respectively. (correct use is respectively, not respectfully)

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: She respectfully greeted her teacher.

Roman Nepali: Unile aafno shikshaklai samman purbak namaste garin.

Meaning: Politely.

#### Example 2

English: He respectfully disagreed.

Roman Nepali: Unile samman purbak asahamat janaye.

Meaning: Polite disagreement.

#### Example 3

English: Respectfully, I have another opinion.

Roman Nepali: Samman purbak, mero arko bichar cha.

Meaning: Courteous expression.

### Memory Trick

**Respectfully = Politely**

---

## Word 2: Respectively

### Simple English Meaning

In the same order mentioned.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Kram anusar

### Use

Lists and comparisons.

### Where to Use

✅ A and B scored 10 and 20 respectively

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Polite behavior

### Structure (if applicable)

..., respectively.

### Common Mistake

❌ He respectfully scored 90 marks.

✅ He scored 90 marks.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Ram and Hari are 15 and 16 years old respectively.

Roman Nepali: Ram ra Hari kram anusar 15 ra 16 barsaka chan.

Meaning: Same order.

#### Example 2

English: The red and blue boxes weighed 5 and 7 kg respectively.

Roman Nepali: Rato ra nilo box kram anusar 5 ra 7 kilo thie.

Meaning: Order maintained.

#### Example 3

English: The winners received gold and silver medals respectively.

Roman Nepali: Bijetaharle kram anusar swarna ra rajat padak paye.

Meaning: Matching order.

### Memory Trick

**Respectively = In order**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Respectfully | Politely | Behavior |
| Respectively | In order | Lists |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. She ______ greeted the principal.
2. Ram and Shyam scored 80 and 90 ______.
3. He ______ disagreed with the decision.

Answers:

1. respectfully
2. respectively
3. respectfully

---

## Quick Revision

* Respectfully = Politely
* Respectively = In order

To study again or make corrections.

Dohoryaera padhnu wa sudhar garnu

### Use

Exams, writing, plans.

### Where to Use

✅ Revise notes

### Where NOT to Use

❌ General evaluation only

### Structure (if applicable)

Revise + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ I reviewed all night for the exam.

✅ I revised all night for the exam.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I revised my notes before the test.

Roman Nepali: Maile pariksha aghi note dohoryaera padhe.

Meaning: Studied again.

#### Example 2

English: She revised the essay.

Roman Nepali: Unile nibandha sudharin.

Meaning: Made corrections.

#### Example 3

English: Students should revise regularly.

Roman Nepali: Bidyarthile niyamit rupma dohoryaera padhnu parchha.

Meaning: Study again.

### Memory Trick

**Revise = Study again**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Review | Check carefully | Reports, work |
| Revise | Study again or correct | Exams, drafts |

## Exam Pattern

2. I need to ______ for tomorrow's exam.
3. The teacher ______ the document.

Answers:

2. revise
3. reviewed

* Review = Check
* Revise = Study again

# 187. Weather / Whether

## Type

Homophone

---

## Word 1: Weather

### Simple English Meaning

The condition of the air outside.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Mausam

### Use

Rain, sun, wind, temperature.

### Where to Use

✅ Good weather

✅ Bad weather

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Choice between options

### Structure (if applicable)

Weather + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ I don't know weather he will come.

✅ I don't know whether he will come.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The weather is sunny.

Roman Nepali: Mausam ghamilo cha.

Meaning: Climate condition.

#### Example 2

English: Bad weather delayed the flight.

Roman Nepali: Kharab mausamle udan dhilo bhayo.

Meaning: Outdoor condition.

#### Example 3

English: We enjoy warm weather.

Roman Nepali: Hami nyano mausam manparauchhau.

Meaning: Climate.

### Memory Trick

**Weather = Mausam**

---

## Word 2: Whether

### Simple English Meaning

Used when talking about a choice or possibility.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Ki

### Use

Choices, uncertainty.

### Where to Use

✅ Whether or not

✅ Whether he comes

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Rain, sun, wind

### Structure (if applicable)

Whether + clause

### Common Mistake

❌ The whether is nice today.

✅ The weather is nice today.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I don't know whether he will come.

Roman Nepali: Uni aauchhan ki aaudainan malai thaha chaina.

Meaning: Uncertain choice.

#### Example 2

English: Decide whether to stay or leave.

Roman Nepali: Basne ki jane nirnaya gara.

Meaning: Choice.

#### Example 3

English: She asked whether I was ready.

Roman Nepali: Unile ma tayar chu ki chaina bhanera sodhin.

Meaning: Possibility.

### Memory Trick

**Whether = Choice**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Weather | Mausam | Climate |
| Whether | Ki | Choice |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The ______ is cold today.
2. I don't know ______ he agrees.
3. Bad ______ affected travel.

Answers:

1. weather
2. whether
3. weather

---

## Quick Revision

* Weather = Mausam
* Whether = Ki

---

Meaning: Who is.

#### Example 3

English: Who's finished the work?

Meaning: Who has.

## Word 2: Whose

Showing ownership.

**Whose = Ownership**

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Who's | Who is / Who has | Questions |

## Exam Pattern

2. ______ phone is ringing?

### Structure (if applicable)

Council + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ He gave good council. (if meaning advice)

✅ He gave good counsel.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The city council approved the project.

Roman Nepali: Sahar parishadle yojana swikrit garyo.

Meaning: Decision-making group.

#### Example 2

English: She joined the student council.

Roman Nepali: Uni bidyarthi parishadma samel bhain.

Meaning: Committee.

#### Example 3

English: The council met yesterday.

Roman Nepali: Parishad hija bhetghat garyo.

Meaning: Official group.

### Memory Trick

**Council = Committee**

---

## Word 2: Counsel

### Simple English Meaning

Advice or guidance.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Sallaha

### Use

Law, guidance, support.

### Where to Use

✅ Give counsel

✅ Legal counsel

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Decision-making committee

### Structure (if applicable)

Counsel + noun/verb

### Common Mistake

❌ The counsel voted on the law.

✅ The council voted on the law.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: She gave wise counsel.

Roman Nepali: Unile buddhiman sallaha diin.

Meaning: Advice.

#### Example 2

English: He sought legal counsel.

Roman Nepali: Unile kanuni sallaha khoje.

Meaning: Professional advice.

#### Example 3

English: Parents should counsel their children.

Roman Nepali: Aama buwale bachchaharu lai margadarshan garnu parchha.

Meaning: Guide.

### Memory Trick

**Counsel = Advice**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Council | Committee | Government |
| Counsel | Advice | Guidance |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The city ______ met today.
2. He gave useful ______.
3. She sought legal ______.

Answers:

1. council
2. counsel
3. counsel

---

## Quick Revision

* Council = Parishad
* Counsel = Sallaha

---

# 199. Defuse / Diffuse

Look-Alike Words

#### Example 3

English: Humor can defuse conflict.

## Exam Pattern

* Defuse = Remove danger

Look-Alike Words

### Use

English: She is an eminent scientist.

#### Example 2

English: The eminent doctor received an award.

#### Example 3

English: We listened to an eminent speaker.

# 202. Ensure / Insure / Assure

## Type

Confusing Words

---

## Word 1: Ensure

### Simple English Meaning

To make certain that something happens.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Nischit garnu

### Use

Results, safety, success.

### Where to Use

✅ Ensure safety

✅ Ensure quality

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Insurance policy

### Structure (if applicable)

Ensure + noun/clause

### Common Mistake

❌ I insured that everyone arrived.

✅ I ensured that everyone arrived.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Please ensure the door is locked.

Roman Nepali: Dhokaa banda cha bhanera nischit gara.

Meaning: Make sure.

#### Example 2

English: We ensure quality service.

Roman Nepali: Hami gunastariya sewa nischit garchhau.

Meaning: Guarantee.

#### Example 3

English: She ensured success through hard work.

Roman Nepali: Unile mehanatle safalta nischit garin.

Meaning: Made certain.

### Memory Trick

**Ensure = Make sure**

---

## Word 2: Insure

### Simple English Meaning

To protect financially through insurance.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Bima garnu

### Use

Cars, houses, health.

### Where to Use

✅ Insure a car

✅ Insure a house

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Make sure

### Structure (if applicable)

Insure + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ I insured that the report was correct.

✅ I ensured that the report was correct.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: We insured the car.

Roman Nepali: Hami le gadi ko bima garyau.

Meaning: Insurance protection.

#### Example 2

English: She insured her house.

Roman Nepali: Unile ghar ko bima garin.

Meaning: Financial coverage.

#### Example 3

English: Many people insure their health.

Roman Nepali: Dherai manchhe le swasthyako bima garchhan.

Meaning: Insurance.

### Memory Trick

**Insure = Insurance**

---

## Word 3: Assure

### Simple English Meaning

To tell someone confidently that something is true.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Biswas dilaaunu

### Use

People, confidence.

### Where to Use

✅ Assure someone

✅ Assure a friend

### Where NOT to Use

### Structure (if applicable)

Assure + person

### Common Mistake

❌ I assured my car.

✅ I insured my car.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I assure you everything is fine.

Roman Nepali: Ma timilai sabai thik cha bhanera biswas dilauchhu.

Meaning: Give confidence.

#### Example 2

English: She assured her parents.

Roman Nepali: Unile aafno aama buwalai biswas dilain.

Meaning: Reassure.

#### Example 3

English: The teacher assured the students.

Roman Nepali: Shikshakle bidyarthilai dhukka banae.

Meaning: Give confidence.

### Memory Trick

**Assure = Tell a person**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Ensure | Make certain | Results |
| Insure | Provide insurance | Property |
| Assure | Give confidence | People |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. We must ______ safety.
2. He ______ his car.
3. I ______ you that all is well.

Answers:

2. insured
3. assure

---

## Quick Revision

* Ensure = Make sure
* Insure = Bima
* Assure = Give confidence

# 203. Farther / Further

Confusing Words

## Word 1: Farther

### Use

✅ Farther away

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Further down the road (often accepted, but farther is better for physical distance)

✅ Farther down the road

#### Example 1

English: His house is farther away.

Roman Nepali: Unko ghar ajhai tada cha.

Meaning: Physical distance.

#### Example 2

English: We walked farther than before.

Meaning: More distance.

#### Example 3

English: The village is farther north.

Roman Nepali: Gaun ajhai uttar tira cha.

Meaning: Measurable distance.

### Memory Trick

**Farther = Distance**

---

## Word 2: Further

### Simple English Meaning

More, additional, or deeper.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Ajhai thap

### Use

Discussion, study, information.

### Where to Use

✅ Further information

✅ Further study

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Mainly physical distance

### Structure (if applicable)

Further + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ I need farther information.

✅ I need further information.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: We need further details.

Roman Nepali: Hamilai ajhai thap bibaran chahinchha.

Meaning: Additional.

#### Example 2

English: Further study is required.

Roman Nepali: Ajhai thap adhyayan awasyak cha.

Meaning: More study.

#### Example 3

English: The matter needs further discussion.

Roman Nepali: Yo bishayma ajhai thap chalfal chahinchha.

Meaning: Additional discussion.

### Memory Trick

**Further = More**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Farther | Physical distance | Places |
| Further | Additional | Information |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The school is ______ away.
2. We need ______ information.
3. They walked ______ than us.

Answers:

2. further
3. farther

---

## Quick Revision

* Farther = Distance
* Further = More

1. The engine remained ______.

2. idol

Look-Alike Words

Honest, innocent, and simple.

People, behavior.

Ingenuous + noun

Meaning: Innocent and sincere.

#### Example 3

English: His ingenuous nature impressed everyone.

Meaning: Honest character.

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|

3. ingenious

---

## Quick Revision

* Ingenious = Clever
* Ingenuous = Innocent

# 210. Later / Latter

## Type

Confusing Words

---

## Word 1: Later

### Simple English Meaning

After the present time.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Pachhi

### Use

Time.

### Where to Use

✅ See you later

✅ Later today

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Second of two things

### Structure (if applicable)

Later + time

### Common Mistake

❌ Of tea and coffee, I prefer the later.

✅ Of tea and coffee, I prefer the latter.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I will call you later.

Roman Nepali: Ma timilai pachhi phone garchhu.

Meaning: After some time.

#### Example 2

English: We will meet later.

Roman Nepali: Hami pachhi bhetnechhau.

Meaning: Future time.

#### Example 3

English: Finish it later.

Roman Nepali: Yaslai pachhi sakau.

Meaning: Not now.

### Memory Trick

**Later = Pachhi**

---

## Word 2: Latter

### Simple English Meaning

The second of two things mentioned.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Dosro kura

### Use

Comparisons between two items.

### Where to Use

✅ The latter option

✅ The latter choice

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Future time

### Structure (if applicable)

Former ... latter

### Common Mistake

❌ I will see you latter.

✅ I will see you later.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Of tea and coffee, I prefer the latter.

Roman Nepali: Chiya ra coffee madhye ma dosro rochchhu.

Meaning: Second item.

#### Example 2

English: The latter idea was better.

Roman Nepali: Dosro bichar ramro thiyo.

Meaning: Second option.

#### Example 3

English: Between the two plans, the latter is cheaper.

Roman Nepali: Dui yojanama dosro sasto cha.

Meaning: Second mentioned thing.

### Memory Trick

**Latter = Last of two**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Later | After now | Time |
| Latter | Second of two | Comparison |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. I will talk to you ______.
2. Between the two options, I chose the ______.
3. We can discuss it ______ today.

Answers:

1. later
2. latter
3. later

---

## Quick Revision

* Later = Pachhi
* Latter = Second of two

Confusing Words

### Use

### Common Mistake

#### Example 3

* Led = Past

# 219. Respectfully / Respectively

Look-Alike Words

---

## Word 1: Respectfully

### Simple English Meaning

### Roman Nepali Meaning

### Use

### Where to Use

✅ Respectfully disagree

✅ Respectfully request

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Match items in order

### Structure (if applicable)

Respectfully + verb

### Common Mistake

❌ Ram and Hari scored 70 and 80 respectively. (correct word is respectively)

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I respectfully disagree.

Roman Nepali: Ma samman purbak asahamat chhu.

Meaning: Polite disagreement.

#### Example 2

English: She respectfully answered.

Roman Nepali: Unile samman purbak jawab diin.

Meaning: Politely.

#### Example 3

English: He spoke respectfully.

Roman Nepali: Uni samman purbak bole.

Meaning: With respect.

### Memory Trick

**Respectfully = Politely**

---

## Word 2: Respectively

### Simple English Meaning

In the same order as mentioned.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Kram anusar

### Use

### Where to Use

✅ A and B scored 50 and 60 respectively.

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Polite speaking

### Structure (if applicable)

List + respectively

### Common Mistake

❌ I respectfully request your help. (respectfully is correct)

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Ram and Hari are 15 and 16 years old respectively.

Meaning: Matching order.

### Memory Trick

**Respectively = Same order**

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Respectfully | Politely | Behavior |
| Respectively | In order | Lists |

## Exam Pattern

2. Ram and Hari scored 60 and 70 ______.

2. respectively
3. respectfully

* Respectfully = Politely
* Respectively = In order

# 220. Rightfully / Rightly

## Type

Confusing Adverbs

---

## Word 1: Rightfully

### Simple English Meaning

According to legal or moral right.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Adhikar anusar

### Use

Ownership, rights.

### Where to Use

✅ Rightfully yours

✅ Rightfully owned

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Simply meaning correctly

### Structure (if applicable)

Rightfully + verb

### Common Mistake

❌ You are rightfully correct.

✅ You are rightly correct.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The land rightfully belongs to her.

Roman Nepali: Jagga unko adhikar anusar ho.

Meaning: Legal right.

#### Example 2

English: He rightfully claimed the prize.

Roman Nepali: Unile adhikar anusar puraskar mage.

Meaning: Deserved.

#### Example 3

English: She was rightfully angry.

Roman Nepali: Uni uchit rupma risayeki thin.

Meaning: Justified.

### Memory Trick

**Rightfully = By right**

---

## Word 2: Rightly

### Simple English Meaning

Correctly or properly.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Sahi rupma

### Use

Opinions, judgments.

### Where to Use

✅ Rightly said

✅ Rightly understood

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Legal ownership

### Structure (if applicable)

Rightly + verb

### Common Mistake

❌ The property rightly belongs to him. (rightfully is stronger)

✅ The property rightfully belongs to him.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: You rightly answered the question.

Roman Nepali: Timile sahi jawab diyau.

Meaning: Correctly.

#### Example 2

English: She rightly predicted the result.

Roman Nepali: Unile sahi anumaan garin.

Meaning: Correct.

#### Example 3

English: He was rightly praised.

Roman Nepali: Unko sahi tarikale prasansa bhayo.

Meaning: Properly deserved.

### Memory Trick

**Rightly = Correctly**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Rightfully | By legal/moral right | Ownership |
| Rightly | Correctly | Judgment |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The property ______ belongs to her.
2. You ______ solved the problem.
3. He ______ claimed the reward.

Answers:

1. rightfully
2. rightly
3. rightfully

---

## Quick Revision

* Rightfully = By right
* Rightly = Correctly

------

# 224. Sew / So / Sow

## Type

Homophones

---

## Word 1: Sew

### Simple English Meaning

To join cloth with needle and thread.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Silaunu

### Use

Clothes, fabric.

### Where to Use

✅ Sew a shirt

✅ Sew a button

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Plant seeds

❌ Therefore

### Structure (if applicable)

Sew + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Farmers sew rice seeds.

✅ Farmers sow rice seeds.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: My mother can sew clothes.

Roman Nepali: Meri aamale luga silauna saknuhunchha.

Meaning: Stitch fabric.

#### Example 2

English: She sewed a button.

Roman Nepali: Unile euta button silaain.

Meaning: Attach with thread.

#### Example 3

English: I want to sew this shirt.

Roman Nepali: Ma yo shirt silauna chahanchhu.

Meaning: Make or repair clothes.

### Memory Trick

**Sew = Stitch**

---

## Word 2: So

### Simple English Meaning

Therefore; for this reason.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Tyasaile

### Use

Connecting ideas.

### Where to Use

✅ I was tired, so I slept.

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Sewing

❌ Planting seeds

### Structure (if applicable)

Clause + so + clause

### Common Mistake

❌ I was tired, sew I slept.

✅ I was tired, so I slept.

Roman Nepali: Pani pariraheko thiyo, tyasaile hami gharmai basyau.

#### Example 2

English: I was hungry, so I ate.

Roman Nepali: Malai bhok lageko thiyo, tyasaile maile khaye.

Meaning: Result.

#### Example 3

English: She studied hard, so she passed.

Roman Nepali: Unile mehanat gari padhin, tyasaile pass bhain.

Meaning: Cause and result.

### Memory Trick

**So = Therefore**

## Word 3: Sow

### Simple English Meaning

To plant seeds in the ground.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

### Use

Farming, gardening.

### Where to Use

### Where NOT to Use

### Structure (if applicable)

### Common Mistake

✅ Please sow the seeds.

### Examples

#### Example 1

Roman Nepali: Kisanharule basantma biu ropchhan.

Meaning: Plant seeds.

#### Example 2

English: We sowed corn last week.

Roman Nepali: Hami le gata hapta makai ropyau.

Meaning: Agricultural planting.

#### Example 3

English: It is time to sow rice.

Roman Nepali: Dhan ropne samaya bhayo.

Meaning: Farming activity.

### Memory Trick

**Sow = Seeds**

---

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|

| So | Therefore | Connection |
| Sow | Plant seeds | Farming |

---

2. She can ______ clothes.
3. I was tired, ______ I rested.

2. sew
3. so

* So = Therefore

**Team = Toli**

## Word 2: Teem

### Simple English Meaning

To be full of something.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Bhari hunu

### Use

Fish, people, activity.

### Where to Use

✅ Teem with life

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Group of workers

### Structure (if applicable)

Teem with + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Our teem won.

✅ Our team won.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The river teems with fish.

Roman Nepali: Nadi machhale bhariyeko cha.

Meaning: Full of.

#### Example 2

English: The market teems with people.

Roman Nepali: Bajarma manchheharu dherai chan.

Meaning: Crowded.

#### Example 3

English: The forest teems with wildlife.

Roman Nepali: Jangal banjantu le bhariyeko cha.

Meaning: Full of living things.

### Memory Trick

**Teem with = Full of**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Team | Group | Sports, work |
| Teem | Be full of | Large quantity |

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. Our ______ won the game.
2. The lake ______ with fish.
3. She joined the school ______.

Answers:

2. teems
3. team

---

## Quick Revision

* Team = Toli
* Teem = Full of

Meaning: Not the same.

Farming, oxen, animals.

### Where to Use

✅ Oxen yoke

✅ Put on a yoke

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Egg part

### Structure (if applicable)

Yoke + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ The yolk was placed on the oxen.

✅ The yoke was placed on the oxen.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The farmer placed a yoke on the oxen.

Roman Nepali: Kisanle goru ma juwa rakhe.

Meaning: Farming tool.

#### Example 2

English: The yoke joined the two animals.

Roman Nepali: Juwale dui janawar jodyo.

Meaning: Connects animals.

#### Example 3

English: The wooden yoke was heavy.

Roman Nepali: Kathero juwa bhari thiyo.

Meaning: Animal equipment.

### Memory Trick

**Yoke = Oxen tool**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Yolk | Yellow egg part | Cooking |
| Yoke | Animal harness | Farming |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The recipe needs one egg ______.
2. The farmer used a ______.
3. Separate the ______ from the egg white.

Answers:

1. yolk
2. yoke
3. yolk

---

## Quick Revision

* Yolk = Egg center
* Yoke = Juwa

# 246. Censor / Censure

## Type

Look-Alike Words

---

## Word 1: Censor

### Simple English Meaning

To remove or hide parts of information that are not allowed.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

### Use

### Where to Use

✅ Censor a report

✅ Government censorship

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Criticize someone officially

### Structure (if applicable)

Censor + noun

### Common Mistake

✅ The committee censured him for misconduct.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Some scenes were censored from the movie.

Roman Nepali: Filmka kehi drishyaharu hataiye.

Meaning: Removed content.

#### Example 2

English: The article was censored.

Roman Nepali: Lekhma rok lagaiyo.

Meaning: Restricted information.

#### Example 3

English: The government censored the broadcast.

Roman Nepali: Sarkarle prasaranma rok lagayo.

Meaning: Controlled content.

### Memory Trick

---

## Word 2: Censure

### Simple English Meaning

To criticize strongly and officially.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Kada alochana garnu

### Use

Organizations, governments, committees.

### Where to Use

✅ Censure a member

✅ Official censure

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Remove information

### Structure (if applicable)

### Common Mistake

✅ The TV channel censored the film.

Meaning: Official criticism.

#### Example 2

English: She faced public censure.

Meaning: Strong disapproval.

#### Example 3

English: The council censured his actions.

Roman Nepali: Parishadle unko karyako alochana garyo.

Meaning: Formal criticism.

### Memory Trick

**Censure = Criticize**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Censor | Remove/restrict content | Media |
| Censure | Official criticism | Organizations |

---

## Exam Pattern

2. The committee ______ the official.
3. Some scenes were ______ from TV.

Answers:

2. censured
3. censored

---

## Quick Revision

* Censor = Restrict content
* Censure = Criticize officially

Look-Alike Words

## Word 1: Defuse

English: She tried to defuse the argument.

#### Example 3

* Defuse = Make safe

### Where NOT to Use

### Structure (if applicable)

Illicit + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ The teacher illicited answers.

✅ The teacher elicited answers.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Police stopped illicit trade.

Roman Nepali: Praharile abaidh byapar roke.

Meaning: Illegal activity.

#### Example 2

English: Illicit drugs are dangerous.

Roman Nepali: Abaidh lagu aushadhi khatarnak hunchhan.

Meaning: Illegal items.

#### Example 3

English: They were involved in illicit business.

Roman Nepali: Uniharu abaidh byaparma samlagna thiye.

Meaning: Unlawful activity.

### Memory Trick

**Illicit = Illegal**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Elicit | Get information | Communication |
| Illicit | Illegal | Law |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The interview ______ useful information.
2. Police seized ______ goods.
3. The question ______ a response.

Answers:

1. elicited
2. illicit
3. elicited

---

## Quick Revision

* Elicit = Obtain
* Illicit = Abaidh

---

# 253. Eminent / Imminent

## Type

Look-Alike Words

---

## Word 1: Eminent

### Simple English Meaning

Famous and respected.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Prasiddha ra sammanit

### Use

People, experts, leaders.

### Where to Use

✅ Eminent scientist

✅ Eminent doctor

### Where NOT to Use

❌ About to happen

### Structure (if applicable)

Eminent + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ An eminent storm is coming.

✅ An imminent storm is coming.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: She is an eminent professor.

Roman Nepali: Uni prasiddha professor hun.

Meaning: Highly respected.

#### Example 2

English: An eminent scientist gave a lecture.

Roman Nepali: Euta prasiddha bigyanikle pravachan diye.

Meaning: Famous expert.

#### Example 3

English: The eminent leader received an award.

Roman Nepali: Sammanit netale puraskar paae.

Meaning: Well-known person.

### Memory Trick

**Eminent = Excellent person**

---

## Word 2: Imminent

### Simple English Meaning

About to happen very soon.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Chittai huna lagheko

### Use

Danger, events, changes.

### Where to Use

✅ Imminent danger

✅ Imminent arrival

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Famous person

### Structure (if applicable)

Imminent + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ He is an imminent scientist.

✅ He is an eminent scientist.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Rain is imminent.

Roman Nepali: Chittai pani parna lagyo.

Meaning: About to happen.

#### Example 2

English: The danger was imminent.

Roman Nepali: Khatara najik thiyo.

Meaning: Very soon.

#### Example 3

English: Their arrival is imminent.

Roman Nepali: Uniharuko aagaman chittai hunchha.

Meaning: Near in time.

### Memory Trick

**Imminent = Immediate soon**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Eminent | Famous and respected | People |
| Imminent | About to happen | Events |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The ______ doctor spoke at the event.
2. A storm is ______.
3. She is an ______ scholar.

Answers:

1. eminent
2. imminent
3. eminent

---

## Quick Revision

* Eminent = Famous
* Imminent = Coming soon

# 254. Ensure / Insure / Assure

## Type

Confusing Words

---

## Word 1: Ensure

### Simple English Meaning

To make certain that something happens.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Nischit garnu

### Use

Results, safety, success.

### Where to Use

✅ Ensure quality

✅ Ensure safety

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Insurance policy

### Structure (if applicable)

Ensure + noun/clause

### Common Mistake

❌ I insured that the door was locked.

✅ I ensured that the door was locked.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Ensure that the door is closed.

Roman Nepali: Dhoka banda cha bhanera nischit gara.

Meaning: Make certain.

#### Example 2

English: We ensure customer satisfaction.

Roman Nepali: Hami grahak santushti nischit garchhau.

Meaning: Guarantee result.

#### Example 3

English: Ensure your work is correct.

Roman Nepali: Aafno kaam thik cha bhanera nischit gara.

Meaning: Verify.

### Memory Trick

**Ensure = Make sure**

---

## Word 2: Insure

### Simple English Meaning

To protect financially through insurance.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Bima garnu

### Use

Cars, houses, health.

### Where to Use

✅ Insure a car

✅ Insure property

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Make sure

### Structure (if applicable)

Insure + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Please insure the report is correct.

✅ Please ensure the report is correct.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: We insured the house.

Roman Nepali: Hami le ghar ko bima garyau.

Meaning: Financial protection.

#### Example 2

English: You should insure your car.

Roman Nepali: Timile aafno gadi ko bima garnu parchha.

Meaning: Insurance coverage.

#### Example 3

English: The company insures workers.

Roman Nepali: Companyle karmachariko bima garchha.

Meaning: Provides insurance.

### Memory Trick

**Insure = Insurance**

---

## Word 3: Assure

### Simple English Meaning

To tell someone confidently that something is true.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Biswas dilau nu

### Use

People, confidence.

### Where to Use

✅ Assure someone

✅ Assure a customer

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Insurance coverage

### Structure (if applicable)

Assure + person

### Common Mistake

❌ I assured my car.

✅ I insured my car.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I assure you everything is fine.

Roman Nepali: Ma timilai sabai thik cha bhanera biswas dilauchhu.

Meaning: Give confidence.

#### Example 2

English: She assured her parents.

Roman Nepali: Unile aama buwalai biswas dilain.

Meaning: Reassure.

#### Example 3

English: The manager assured customers.

Roman Nepali: Managerle grahaklai biswas dilaye.

Meaning: Give confidence.

### Memory Trick

**Assure = Tell a person**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Ensure | Make certain | Results |
| Insure | Provide insurance | Property |
| Assure | Give confidence | People |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. Please ______ that all doors are locked.
2. We ______ our house last year.
3. I ______ you everything is okay.

Answers:

1. ensure
2. insured
3. assure

---

## Quick Revision

* Ensure = Make sure
* Insure = Bima garnu
* Assure = Biswas dilau nu

---

# 255. Farther / Further

## Type

Confusing Words

---

## Word 1: Farther

### Simple English Meaning

A greater physical distance.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Ajhai tada

### Use

Roads, places, measurable distance.

### Where to Use

✅ Farther away

✅ Farther down the road

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Additional discussion or ideas

### Structure (if applicable)

Farther than

### Common Mistake

❌ We need farther information.

✅ We need further information.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The school is farther than I thought.

Roman Nepali: School maile socheko bhanda tada cha.

Meaning: Physical distance.

#### Example 2

English: Walk farther down the road.

Roman Nepali: Batoma ajhai agadi hid.

Meaning: More distance.

#### Example 3

English: The village is farther away.

Roman Nepali: Gau ajhai tada cha.

Meaning: Location.

### Memory Trick

**Farther = Distance**

---

## Word 2: Further

### Simple English Meaning

More, additional, or to a greater degree.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Ajhai thap

### Use

Information, discussion, progress.

### Where to Use

✅ Further details

✅ Further study

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Measurable distance (usually)

### Structure (if applicable)

Further + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ The shop is further than the park. (physical distance preferred: farther)

✅ The shop is farther than the park.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: We need further information.

Roman Nepali: Hamilai ajhai thap jankari chahincha.

Meaning: Additional.

#### Example 2

English: Further discussion is needed.

Roman Nepali: Ajhai thap chalfal awasyak cha.

Meaning: More.

#### Example 3

English: She took further training.

Roman Nepali: Unile ajhai thap talim lin.

Meaning: Additional learning.

### Memory Trick

**Further = More**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Farther | Greater distance | Places |
| Further | Additional | Information |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The village is ______ away.
2. We need ______ details.
3. Walk ______ along the road.

Answers:

1. farther
2. further
3. farther

---

## Quick Revision

* Farther = Physical distance
* Further = Additional

------

### Where NOT to Use

### Structure (if applicable)

### Common Mistake

English: The agreement was historic.

#### Example 3

English: We visited a historic site.

# 259. Later / Latter

## Type

Confusing Words

---

## Word 1: Later

### Simple English Meaning

After some time.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Pachhi

### Use

Time, future events.

### Where to Use

✅ See you later

✅ Later today

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Second of two things

### Structure (if applicable)

Later + time expression

### Common Mistake

❌ Of tea and coffee, I prefer later.

✅ Of tea and coffee, I prefer the latter.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I will call you later.

Roman Nepali: Ma timilai pachhi phone garchhu.

Meaning: Future time.

#### Example 2

English: We will meet later.

Roman Nepali: Hami pachhi bhetnechhau.

Meaning: After some time.

#### Example 3

English: Later that day, it rained.

Roman Nepali: Tyo din pachhi pani paryo.

Meaning: Afterwards.

### Memory Trick

**Later = Time**

---

## Word 2: Latter

### Simple English Meaning

The second of two things mentioned.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Dosro kura

### Use

Comparisons, choices.

### Where to Use

✅ The latter option

✅ The latter of two

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Future time

### Structure (if applicable)

The latter

### Common Mistake

❌ I will come latter.

✅ I will come later.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Tea and coffee are available; I prefer the latter.

Roman Nepali: Chiya ra coffee cha; malai dosro manparcha.

Meaning: Second option.

#### Example 2

English: Of the two plans, the latter is better.

Roman Nepali: Dui yojanama dosro ramro cha.

Meaning: Second one.

#### Example 3

English: The latter choice was cheaper.

Roman Nepali: Dosro rojai sasto thiyo.

Meaning: Mentioned second.

### Memory Trick

**Latter = Last mentioned**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Later | After some time | Time |
| Latter | Second of two | Comparison |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. I will visit you ______.
2. Of the two books, I prefer the ______.
3. We can discuss it ______ today.

Answers:

2. latter
3. later

---

## Quick Revision

* Later = Pachhi
* Latter = Second of two

## Word 1: Pore

### Use

### Where to Use

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Not having enough money

### Structure (if applicable)

### Common Mistake

✅ Pore over your books.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: She pored over her notes.

Meaning: Studied carefully.

#### Example 2

English: He pored over the report.

Meaning: Examined closely.

#### Example 3

English: Students pore over books before exams.

Meaning: Careful study.

## Word 2: Pour

To make liquid flow from one container to another.

### Use

Water, tea, juice.

### Where to Use

✅ Pour water

✅ Pour tea

### Where NOT to Use

### Structure (if applicable)

Meaning: Put liquid.

#### Example 3

English: Please pour some juice.

Roman Nepali: Kripaya ali juice khanyaau.

Meaning: Flow liquid.

## Word 3: Poor

### Simple English Meaning

Having little money; not good quality.

Garib; naramro

### Use

Money, quality, condition.

### Where to Use

✅ Poor family

✅ Poor result

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Liquid action

Poor + noun

Meaning: Little money.

#### Example 2

English: He got poor marks.

Meaning: Low quality result.

#### Example 3

Meaning: Not good.

**Poor = No money / not good**

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Pore | Study carefully | Reading |
| Pour | Let liquid flow | Drinks |
| Poor | Not rich / not good | Condition |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. Students ______ over books before exams.
2. ______ the juice into the glass.
3. The village is very ______.

Answers:

1. pore
2. pour
3. poor

---

## Quick Revision

* Pore = Study carefully
* Pour = Liquid flow
* Poor = Garib / not good
