# 263. Precede / Proceed

## Type

Look-Alike Words

---

## Word 1: Precede

### Simple English Meaning

To come before something.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Agadi aunu

### Use

Events, words, actions.

### Where to Use

✅ Precede an event

✅ Precede a speech

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Continue forward

### Structure (if applicable)

Precede + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Please precede with the meeting.

✅ Please proceed with the meeting.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: A short speech preceded the ceremony.

Roman Nepali: Samaroh aghi sano bhashan bhayo.

Meaning: Came before.

#### Example 2

English: Thunder often precedes rain.

Roman Nepali: Garjan prayah pani aghi hunchha.

Meaning: Happens first.

#### Example 3

English: The introduction precedes the main chapter.

Roman Nepali: Parichaya mukhya adhyay aghi aauchha.

Meaning: Comes before.

### Memory Trick

**Precede = Previous**

---

## Word 2: Proceed

### Simple English Meaning

To continue or move forward.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Agadi badhnu

### Use

Meetings, work, travel.

### Where to Use

✅ Proceed carefully

✅ Proceed with work

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Come before

### Structure (if applicable)

Proceed with + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Rain proceeded thunder.

✅ Rain preceded thunder.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Please proceed with the project.

Roman Nepali: Kripaya yojana agadi badhau.

Meaning: Continue.

#### Example 2

English: The officer told us to proceed.

Roman Nepali: Adhikarile agadi badhna bhane.

Meaning: Move forward.

#### Example 3

English: We proceeded to the next stage.

Roman Nepali: Hami arko charanma gyaun.

Meaning: Continued.

### Memory Trick

**Proceed = Progress**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Precede | Come before | Order |
| Proceed | Continue | Action |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The speech ______ the event.
2. Please ______ with the plan.
3. The introduction ______ the chapter.

Answers:

1. preceded
2. proceed
3. precedes

---

## Quick Revision

* Precede = Come before
* Proceed = Continue

---

# 264. Prescribe / Proscribe

## Type

Look-Alike Words

---

## Word 1: Prescribe

### Simple English Meaning

To officially recommend or order something.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Nirdesh dinu

### Use

Doctors, rules, treatments.

### Where to Use

✅ Prescribe medicine

✅ Prescribe treatment

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Ban something

### Structure (if applicable)

Prescribe + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ The law prescribed the activity. (if meaning banned)

✅ The law proscribed the activity.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The doctor prescribed medicine.

Roman Nepali: Daktar le aushadhi nirdesh gare.

Meaning: Official recommendation.

#### Example 2

English: Rest was prescribed.

Roman Nepali: Aaram garnu bhanera nirdesh diyo.

Meaning: Ordered treatment.

#### Example 3

English: The rules prescribe safety measures.

Roman Nepali: Niyamle suraksha upay nirdesh garchha.

Meaning: Officially require.

### Memory Trick

**Prescribe = Provide instructions**

---

## Word 2: Proscribe

### Simple English Meaning

To officially forbid or ban.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Pratibandh lagaunu

### Use

Laws, governments.

### Where to Use

✅ Proscribe activities

✅ Proscribe organizations

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Recommend medicine

### Structure (if applicable)

Proscribe + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ The doctor proscribed medicine.

✅ The doctor prescribed medicine.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The government proscribed the group.

Roman Nepali: Sarkarle samuha mathi pratibandh lagayo.

Meaning: Official ban.

#### Example 2

English: Certain activities are proscribed.

Roman Nepali: Kehi kriyakalap pratibandhit chan.

Meaning: Forbidden.

#### Example 3

English: The law proscribes corruption.

Roman Nepali: Kanunle bhrastachar pratibandh garchha.

Meaning: Officially forbids.

### Memory Trick

**Proscribe = Prohibit**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Prescribe | Recommend | Medicine |
| Proscribe | Ban | Law |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The doctor ______ medicine.
2. The law ______ corruption.
3. Rest was ______ by the doctor.

Answers:

2. proscribes
3. prescribed

---

## Quick Revision

* Prescribe = Recommend
* Proscribe = Ban

# 265. Respectfully / Respectively

## Type

Confusing Words

---

## Word 1: Respectfully

### Simple English Meaning

In a polite and respectful way.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Sammanpurbak

### Use

Letters, speaking, requests.

### Where to Use

✅ Respectfully ask

✅ Respectfully disagree

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Match items in order

### Structure (if applicable)

Respectfully + verb

### Common Mistake

❌ Ram and Hari scored 80 and 90 respectively. (correct)
❌ Ram and Hari scored 80 and 90 respectfully.

✅ Ram and Hari scored 80 and 90 respectively.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I respectfully disagree.

Roman Nepali: Ma sammanpurbak asahamat chu.

Meaning: Polite disagreement.

#### Example 2

English: She respectfully answered.

Roman Nepali: Unile sammanpurbak jawab diin.

Meaning: Polite response.

#### Example 3

English: He spoke respectfully.

Roman Nepali: Uni sammanpurbak bole.

Meaning: With respect.

### Memory Trick

**Respectfully = Politely**

---

## Word 2: Respectively

### Simple English Meaning

In the same order as mentioned.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Kram anusaar

### Use

Lists, comparisons.

### Where to Use

✅ A and B scored 10 and 20 respectively.

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Polite speech

### Structure (if applicable)

List + respectively

### Common Mistake

❌ I respectfully request your help. (correct for politely)
❌ I respectively request your help.

✅ I respectfully request your help.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Ram and Hari are 15 and 16 years old respectively.

Roman Nepali: Ram ra Hari kram anusaar 15 ra 16 barsa ka chan.

Meaning: Same order.

#### Example 2

English: The prices were $5 and $10 respectively.

Roman Nepali: Mulya kram anusaar 5 ra 10 dollar thiyo.

Meaning: Corresponding order.

#### Example 3

English: The winners came first and second respectively.

Roman Nepali: Jitneharu kram anusaar pahilo ra dosro aaye.

Meaning: In order.

### Memory Trick

**Respectively = In order**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Respectfully | Politely | Behavior |
| Respectively | In order | Lists |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. I ______ request your support.
2. Ram and Hari scored 70 and 80 ______.
3. She spoke ______ to her teacher.

Answers:

1. respectfully
2. respectively
3. respectfully

---

## Quick Revision

* Respectfully = Politely
* Respectively = Kram anusaar

------
# 266. Rise / Raise

## Type

Confusing Verbs

---

## Word 1: Rise

### Simple English Meaning

To go up by itself.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Mathi uthnu

### Use

Sun, prices, temperature, people.

### Where to Use

✅ The sun rises

✅ Prices rise

✅ Stand up

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Lift something

### Structure (if applicable)

Subject + rise

(No object needed)

### Common Mistake

❌ He rose the box.

✅ He raised the box.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The sun rises in the east.

Roman Nepali: Gham purbabat uthchha.

Meaning: Goes up naturally.

#### Example 2

English: Prices are rising.

Roman Nepali: Mulya badhdai chan.

Meaning: Increasing.

#### Example 3

English: She rose from her chair.

Roman Nepali: Uni kursibata uthin.

Meaning: Stood up.

### Memory Trick

**Rise = Go up by itself**

---

## Word 2: Raise

### Simple English Meaning

To lift or move something upward.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Mathi uthaunu

### Use

Hands, money, children.

### Where to Use

✅ Raise your hand

✅ Raise funds

✅ Raise children

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Something going up naturally

### Structure (if applicable)

Raise + object

### Common Mistake

❌ The sun raises in the east.

✅ The sun rises in the east.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Raise your hand.

Roman Nepali: Aafno haat uthaau.

Meaning: Lift something.

#### Example 2

English: They raised money for charity.

Roman Nepali: Uniharule daanko lagi paisa uthae.

Meaning: Collect.

#### Example 3

English: She raised three children.

Roman Nepali: Unile tin jana bachcha hurkaain.

Meaning: Bring up.

### Memory Trick

**Raise = Lift something**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Rise | Go up itself | Sun, prices |
| Raise | Lift something | Hands, objects |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. Please ______ your hand.
2. The sun ______ every morning.
3. Prices continue to ______.

Answers:

1. raise
2. rises
3. rise

---

## Quick Revision

* Rise = Uthnu
* Raise = Uthaunu

Meaning: Made clothing.

Therefore; very.

#### Example 2

Meaning: Agriculture.

#### Example 3

English: Sow the seeds evenly.

Meaning: Planting.

**Sow = Seeds**

## Comparison Summary

Roman Nepali: Unile ramailo katha sunain.

Meaning: Tale.

#### Example 2

English: I read a story yesterday.

Roman Nepali: Maile hijo euta katha padhe.

Meaning: Written tale.

#### Example 3

English: The movie has a good story.

Roman Nepali: Filmko ramro katha cha.

Meaning: Plot.

### Memory Trick

**Story = Katha**

---

## Word 2: Storey

### Simple English Meaning

A level or floor in a building.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Talla

### Use

Buildings, houses.

### Where to Use

✅ Two-storey house

✅ Three-storey building

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Tale or book content

### Structure (if applicable)

Number + storey

### Common Mistake

❌ I enjoyed the storey.

✅ I enjoyed the story.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: They live in a two-storey house.

Roman Nepali: Uniharu dui talle gharmā baschhan.

Meaning: Building level.

#### Example 2

English: The building has six storeys.

Roman Nepali: Buildingma chha tella chan.

Meaning: Floors.

#### Example 3

English: A new three-storey hotel opened.

Roman Nepali: Naya tin talle hotel khulyo.

Meaning: Building height.

### Memory Trick

**Storey = Storeys in a building**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Story | Tale | Books, movies |
| Storey | Floor of building | Buildings |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. She wrote a short ______.
2. The hotel has five ______.
3. The movie's ______ was interesting.

Answers:

1. story
2. storeys
3. story

---

## Quick Revision

* Story = Katha
* Storey = Talla

### Use

Plans, clothes, arrangements.

✅ Alter a plan

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Religious table

### Structure (if applicable)

Meaning: Change.

#### Example 2

English: She altered her dress.

Roman Nepali: Unile aafno luga pariwartan garin.

#### Example 3

Meaning: Changed.

**Alter = Change**

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Altar | Religious place | Worship |
| Alter | Change | Modification |

* Alter = Change

❌ Musical notes

### Structure (if applicable)

Cord + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Play this cord on the piano.

✅ Play this chord on the piano.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The power cord is damaged.

Roman Nepali: Bijuliko taar bigreko cha.

Meaning: Electrical wire.

#### Example 2

English: Tie it with a cord.

Roman Nepali: Dorile baandha.

Meaning: String or rope.

#### Example 3

English: The cord was unplugged.

Roman Nepali: Taar nikalieko thiyo.

Meaning: Electric cable.

### Memory Trick

**Cord = Cable**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Chord | Musical notes together | Music |
| Cord | Rope or wire | Electricity |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. He played a guitar ______.
2. The power ______ is broken.
3. Learn a new piano ______.

Answers:

1. chord
2. cord
3. chord

---

## Quick Revision

* Chord = Musical notes
* Cord = Dori / taar

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Elicit | Get information/reaction | Communication |
| Illicit | Illegal | Law |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The interview helped ______ useful information.

3. The speech ______ applause.

Answers:

1. elicit
2. illicit
3. elicited

---

## Quick Revision

* Elicit = Jankari nikalnu
* Illicit = Abaidha

# 307. Emerge / Immerse

## Type

Look-Alike Words

---

## Word 1: Emerge

### Simple English Meaning

To come out or become visible.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Bahir niskinu

### Use

Water, darkness, situations.

### Where to Use

✅ Emerge from water

✅ New facts emerge

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Deep involvement

### Structure (if applicable)

Emerge from + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Immerse from the cave.

✅ Emerge from the cave.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The sun emerged from the clouds.

Roman Nepali: Gham badalbata bahira aayo.

Meaning: Became visible.

#### Example 2

English: New evidence emerged.

Roman Nepali: Naya praman dekha paryo.

Meaning: Appeared.

#### Example 3

English: The swimmer emerged from the water.

Roman Nepali: Paudi khelne manchhe panibata bahira aayo.

Meaning: Came out.

### Memory Trick

**Emerge = Exit**

---

## Word 2: Immerse

### Simple English Meaning

To put deeply into something; to become fully involved.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Dubnu / purai lagna

### Use

Water, studies, work.

### Where to Use

✅ Immerse in water

✅ Immerse yourself in study

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Come out

### Structure (if applicable)

Immerse in + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ He emerged himself in the book.

✅ He immersed himself in the book.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Immerse the cloth in water.

Roman Nepali: Kapada panima dubau.

Meaning: Put into liquid.

#### Example 2

English: She immersed herself in study.

Roman Nepali: Uni padhai ma purai lagin.

Meaning: Deep involvement.

#### Example 3

English: The tea bag was immersed in hot water.

Roman Nepali: Chiyako jhola tato panima dubaiyo.

Meaning: Fully covered.

### Memory Trick

**Immerse = Inside deeply**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Emerge | Come out | Visibility |
| Immerse | Go deeply into | Involvement |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The swimmer ______ from the water.
2. She ______ herself in reading.
3. New facts ______ every day.

Answers:

1. emerged
2. immersed
3. emerge

---

## Quick Revision

* Emerge = Bahira niskinu
* Immerse = Dubnu

---

## Exam Pattern

# 310. Farther / Further

## Type

Confusing Words

---

## Word 1: Farther

### Simple English Meaning

At a greater physical distance.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Ajha tadha

### Use

Measurable distance.

### Where to Use

✅ Walk farther

✅ Farther away

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Additional discussion or ideas

### Structure (if applicable)

Farther + distance

### Common Mistake

❌ We need farther discussion.

✅ We need further discussion.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The school is farther away.

Roman Nepali: School ajhai tadha cha.

Meaning: Physical distance.

#### Example 2

English: He ran farther than me.

Roman Nepali: U ma bhanda tadha kudyo.

Meaning: Distance measured.

#### Example 3

English: The village is farther north.

Roman Nepali: Gaun ajhai uttar tira cha.

Meaning: Location distance.

### Memory Trick

**Farther = Far distance**

---

## Word 2: Further

### Simple English Meaning

More, additional, or extra.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Ajhai thap

### Use

Discussion, information, progress.

### Where to Use

✅ Further information

✅ Further study

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Exact physical distance (formal rule)

### Structure (if applicable)

Further + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ The park is further than the school. (acceptable in modern English but farther is more precise)

✅ The park is farther than the school.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: We need further information.

Roman Nepali: Hamilai ajhai thap jankari chahincha.

Meaning: More information.

#### Example 2

English: Further study is required.

Roman Nepali: Ajhai thap adhyayan chahincha.

Meaning: Additional work.

#### Example 3

English: Please explain further.

Roman Nepali: Kripaya ajhai bistar garera bhana.

Meaning: More detail.

### Memory Trick

**Further = More**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Farther | Greater distance | Physical space |
| Further | More/additional | Ideas, information |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The shop is ______ down the road.
2. We need ______ details.
3. He walked ______ than his friend.

Answers:

1. farther
2. further
3. farther

---

## Quick Revision

* Farther = Physical distance
* Further = More / additional

------

Homophone

---

## Word 1: Faze

### Simple English Meaning

### Roman Nepali Meaning

### Use

### Where to Use

### Where NOT to Use

### Structure (if applicable)

Faze + person

✅ We are in the final phase of the project.

Meaning: Became disturbed.

#### Example 3

English: Difficult questions never faze her.

Meaning: Cause worry.

A stage or part of a process.

### Where to Use

### Where NOT to Use

### Common Mistake

#### Example 1

English: The project entered a new phase.

Meaning: New stage.

#### Example 2

English: We completed the first phase.

Meaning: Part of process.

#### Example 3

English: Childhood is an important phase.

Roman Nepali: Balyakal ek mahatwapurna charan ho.

Meaning: Life stage.

### Memory Trick

**Phase = Stage**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Faze | Disturb | Emotions |
| Phase | Stage | Process |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. We are entering a new ______.
2. The criticism did not ______ him.
3. The final ______ begins tomorrow.

Answers:

2. faze
3. phase

---

## Quick Revision

* Faze = Bichalit parnu
* Phase = Charan

❌ Playfully jump around

### Structure (if applicable)

Gamble on + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ The lambs gambled in the field.

✅ The lambs gambolled in the field.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: He gambled all his savings.

Roman Nepali: Usle aafno sabai bachat jokhimma rakhe.

Meaning: Risk money.

#### Example 2

English: Investing blindly is a gamble.

Roman Nepali: Sochi nabichari lagani garnu jokhim ho.

Meaning: Risk.

#### Example 3

English: They gambled on the outcome.

Roman Nepali: Uniharule parinamko jokhim liye.

Meaning: Bet.

### Memory Trick

**Gamble = Risk**

---

## Word 2: Gambol

### Simple English Meaning

To run and jump about playfully.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Ramailo para le ufrinu

### Use

Animals, children.

### Where to Use

✅ Lambs gambol

✅ Children gambol

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Betting money

### Structure (if applicable)

Gambol + around

### Common Mistake

❌ The children gambled around the park.

✅ The children gambolled around the park.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The lambs gambolled in the meadow.

Roman Nepali: Bhedaka bachchaharu khetma ramailo gari ufre.

Meaning: Playful jumping.

#### Example 2

English: Children gambolled happily.

Roman Nepali: Bachchaharu khusile kude.

Meaning: Playful movement.

#### Example 3

English: Puppies gambol in the yard.

Roman Nepali: Kukurka bachchaharu aanganma khelchhan.

Meaning: Frolic.

### Memory Trick

**Gambol = Playfully jump**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Gamble | Risk money | Betting |
| Gambol | Playfully jump | Animals, children |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. He likes to ______ on sports.
2. The lambs ______ in the field.
3. Investing can be a ______.

Answers:

1. gamble
2. gambol
3. gamble

---

## Quick Revision

* Gamble = Jokhim linu
* Gambol = Ufrinu

**Hew = Hack or chop**

---

## Word 2: Hue

### Simple English Meaning

A color or shade of color.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Rangko chhaya

### Use

Art, painting, design.

### Where to Use

✅ Blue hue

✅ Bright hue

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Cutting wood

### Structure (if applicable)

Hue + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ He liked the green hew.

✅ He liked the green hue.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The sky had a golden hue.

Roman Nepali: Aakashma sunaulo rangko chhaya thiyo.

Meaning: Color tone.

#### Example 2

English: The painting uses warm hues.

Roman Nepali: Chitrama nyano rang prayog bhayeko cha.

Meaning: Shades of color.

#### Example 3

English: The flower has a pink hue.

Roman Nepali: Phoolma gulabi rangko chhaya cha.

Meaning: Color variation.

### Memory Trick

**Hue = Color**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Hew | Cut/chop | Woodwork |
| Hue | Color shade | Art |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The carpenter will ______ the wood.
2. The painting has a bright ______.
3. Workers ______ the stone blocks.

Answers:

1. hew
2. hue
3. hewed

---

## Quick Revision

* Hew = Kaatnu
* Hue = Rangko chhaya

---

# 330. Hi / High

## Type

Homophone (in many accents)

## Word 1: Hi

### Simple English Meaning

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Namaste / hello

### Use

Conversation, messages.

### Where to Use

### Where NOT to Use

Meaning: Greeting.

#### Example 2

English: She said hi to me.

Meaning: Friendly welcome.

#### Example 3

English: I waved and said hi.

Roman Nepali: Maile haat hallae ra hello bhane.

Meaning: Greeting someone.

**Hi = Hello**

## Word 2: High

At a great height; above normal level.

Aglo / mathi

Mountains, prices, levels.

### Where NOT to Use

### Structure (if applicable)

### Common Mistake

#### Example 1

#### Example 2

English: Prices are high.

#### Example 3

English: She got a high score.

**High = Height**

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Hi | Greeting | Conversation |
| High | Tall/elevated | Height, levels |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. ______, nice to meet you.
2. The building is very ______.
3. She got a ______ score.

2. high

## Quick Revision

* Hi = Hello
* High = Aglo / mathi

# 331. Hoard / Horde

Homophone

---

## Word 1: Hoard

### Simple English Meaning

### Where NOT to Use

### Structure (if applicable)

### Common Mistake

#### Example 1

Meaning: Store excessively.

#### Example 2

English: He hoarded old newspapers.

Roman Nepali: Usle purana patrika jamma garyo.

Meaning: Collect and keep.

#### Example 3

English: They hoarded gold for years.

Roman Nepali: Uniharule barsau samma sun jamma gare.

Meaning: Stockpile.

### Memory Trick

**Hoard = Store**

## Word 2: Horde

### Simple English Meaning

A large group or crowd of people or animals.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Thulo samuha

### Use

Crowds, groups.

### Where to Use

### Where NOT to Use

### Structure (if applicable)

Horde of + noun

### Common Mistake

✅ He hoarded money.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: A horde of tourists entered the temple.

Roman Nepali: Paryatakharuko thulo samuha mandirma pasyo.

Meaning: Large crowd.

#### Example 2

English: A horde of locusts attacked crops.

Roman Nepali: Tiddiharuko thulo samuha le bali nasta garyo.

Meaning: Large group.

#### Example 3

English: Fans arrived in hordes.

Roman Nepali: Prashansakharu thulo sankhyama aaye.

Meaning: Crowd.

### Memory Trick

**Horde = Huge crowd**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Hoard | Store excessively | Things |
| Horde | Large crowd | People/animals |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. He likes to ______ old coins.
2. A ______ of visitors entered the museum.
3. Families began to ______ food supplies.

Answers:

1. hoard
2. horde
3. hoard

---

## Quick Revision

* Hoard = Jamma garnu
* Horde = Thulo samuha

# 336. Incite / Insight

## Type

Look-Alike Words

---

## Word 1: Incite

### Simple English Meaning

To encourage or provoke someone to do something, especially something harmful.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Uksaunu

### Use

Actions, violence, protests, behavior.

### Where to Use

✅ Incite violence

✅ Incite rebellion

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Deep understanding

### Structure (if applicable)

Incite + noun/object

### Common Mistake

❌ She gave useful incite into the problem.

✅ She gave useful insight into the problem.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The speech incited anger.

Roman Nepali: Bhashanle ris uksayo.

Meaning: Provoked emotion.

#### Example 2

English: He was accused of inciting violence.

Roman Nepali: U hinsa uksayeko aaropma paryo.

Meaning: Encourage harmful action.

#### Example 3

English: False rumors can incite panic.

Roman Nepali: Jhoota halla le aatanka failauna sakchha.

Meaning: Cause reaction.

### Memory Trick

**Incite = Ignite action**

---

## Word 2: Insight

### Simple English Meaning

A deep understanding of something.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Gahiro bujhai

### Use

Knowledge, analysis, learning.

### Where to Use

✅ Valuable insight

✅ Gain insight

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Encourage wrongdoing

### Structure (if applicable)

Insight into + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ His insight violence caused trouble.

✅ His incitement of violence caused trouble.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The book provides insight into history.

Roman Nepali: Kitable itihasma gahiro bujhai dinchha.

Meaning: Understanding.

#### Example 2

English: She has great insight into human behavior.

Roman Nepali: Unisanga manab byabahar ko gahiro bujhai cha.

Meaning: Deep knowledge.

#### Example 3

English: The report offers useful insights.

Roman Nepali: Prativedanle upayogi bujhai dinchha.

Meaning: Understanding.

### Memory Trick

**Insight = Inner sight**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Incite | Provoke | Actions |
| Insight | Deep understanding | Knowledge |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The article provides valuable ______.
2. Rumors can ______ panic.
3. She has great ______ into the issue.

Answers:

1. insight
2. incite
3. insight

---

## Quick Revision

* Incite = Uksaunu
* Insight = Gahiro bujhai

### Use

### Where NOT to Use

### Common Mistake

### Examples

English: He became jealous of his friend.

#### Example 2

English: She was jealous of the attention.

Meaning: Protective feeling.

#### Example 3

English: Jealousy can harm relationships.

Meaning: Possessive feeling.

## Word 2: Envious

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Irshyalu

### Use

### Where NOT to Use

Meaning: Want what others have.

Meaning: Admiration mixed with desire.

#### Example 3

English: He felt envious of their house.

Roman Nepali: U uniharuko ghar dekhera irshya garyo.

Meaning: Wants similar thing.

### Memory Trick

**Envious = Want others' success**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Jealous | Fear of losing | Relationships |
| Envious | Want what others have | Possessions |

---

## Exam Pattern

1. She was ______ of his promotion.
2. He became ______ when she talked to others.
3. Many people are ______ of her talent.

2. jealous
3. envious

---

## Quick Revision

* Jealous = Daha
* Envious = Irshya

✅ You need more practice.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: I need your help.

Roman Nepali: Malai timro sahayog chahincha.

Meaning: Require.

#### Example 2

English: We need more time.

Roman Nepali: Hamilai ajhai samay chahincha.

Meaning: Necessity.

#### Example 3

English: Plants need water.

Roman Nepali: Biruwalai pani chahincha.

Meaning: Requirement.

### Memory Trick

**Need = Require**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Knead | Work dough | Baking |
| Need | Require | Daily life |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. I ______ more information.
2. ______ the dough carefully.
3. Plants ______ sunlight.

Answers:

1. need
2. knead
3. need

* Knead = Pitho mochhnu
* Need = Chahinu

#### Example 3

1. She was ______ to admit the mistake.
2. I ______ cheating.
3. He is ______ to move abroad.

Answers:

1. loath
2. loathe
3. loath

---

## Quick Revision

* Loath = Man nalagne
* Loathe = Ghrina garnu

---

# 352. Loose / Lose

## Type

Confusing Words

---

## Word 1: Loose

### Simple English Meaning

Not tight; free.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Khukulo

### Use

Clothes, ropes, objects.

### Where to Use

✅ Loose shirt

✅ Loose screw

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Fail to keep something

### Structure (if applicable)

Loose + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ Don't loose your keys.

✅ Don't lose your keys.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: This shirt is too loose.

Roman Nepali: Yo shirt dherai khukulo cha.

Meaning: Not tight.

#### Example 2

English: The screw became loose.

Roman Nepali: Petch khukulo bhayo.

Meaning: Not firmly fixed.

#### Example 3

English: Keep the rope loose.

Roman Nepali: Dori khukulo rakha.

Meaning: Free.

### Memory Trick

**Loose = Extra "o" = extra space**

---

## Word 2: Lose

### Simple English Meaning

To fail to keep, find, or win something.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Gumaunu

### Use

Games, objects, opportunities.

### Where to Use

✅ Lose money

✅ Lose a match

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Not tight

### Structure (if applicable)

Lose + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ My pants are lose.

✅ My pants are loose.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Don't lose your phone.

Roman Nepali: Aafno phone nagumau.

Meaning: Misplace.

#### Example 2

English: They may lose the game.

Roman Nepali: Uniharu khel harna sakchhan.

Meaning: Fail to win.

#### Example 3

English: She lost her wallet.

Roman Nepali: Unile aafno purse gumain.

Meaning: Could not find.

### Memory Trick

**Lose = Gumaunu**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Loose | Not tight | Objects |
| Lose | Misplace/fail to win | Actions |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. Don't ______ your passport.
2. The bolt is ______.
3. We might ______ the match.

Answers:

1. lose
2. loose
3. lose

---

## Quick Revision

* Loose = Khukulo
* Lose = Gumaunu

---

# 353. Loot / Lute

## Type

Homophone

---

## Word 1: Loot

### Simple English Meaning

To steal goods, especially during war, riots, or disasters.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Lutnu

### Use

Crime, history.

### Where to Use

✅ Loot a shop

✅ Loot property

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Musical instrument

### Structure (if applicable)

Loot + object

### Common Mistake

❌ He plays the loot.

✅ He plays the lute.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: Thieves looted the store.

Roman Nepali: Chorharule pasal lute.

Meaning: Steal by force.

#### Example 2

English: The museum was looted.

Roman Nepali: Sangrahalaya lutiyo.

Meaning: Robbed.

#### Example 3

English: Rioters looted several buildings.

Roman Nepali: Dangakarile dherai bhawan lute.

Meaning: Steal during unrest.

### Memory Trick

**Loot = Lutnu**

---

## Word 2: Lute

### Simple English Meaning

A stringed musical instrument.

### Roman Nepali Meaning

Tarko baaja

### Use

Music, history.

### Where to Use

✅ Play a lute

✅ Ancient lute

### Where NOT to Use

❌ Robbery

### Structure (if applicable)

Lute + noun

### Common Mistake

❌ They lute the bank.

✅ They loot the bank.

### Examples

#### Example 1

English: The musician played the lute.

Roman Nepali: Sangitkarle lute bajayo.

Meaning: Musical instrument.

#### Example 2

English: The lute was popular in Europe.

Roman Nepali: Europema lute lokpriya thiyo.

Meaning: Historical instrument.

#### Example 3

English: The lute has strings.

Roman Nepali: Lutema taar hunchha.

Meaning: Instrument.

### Memory Trick

**Lute = Music**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning | Use |
|------|---------|------|
| Loot | Steal | Crime |
| Lute | Musical instrument | Music |

---

## Exam Pattern

Fill in the blank:

1. The robbers ______ the warehouse.
2. She learned to play the ______.
3. The soldiers did not ______ the village.

Answers:

1. looted
2. lute
3. loot

---

## Quick Revision

* Loot = Lutnu
* Lute = Baaja

Roman Nepali: Bigyaniharule samadhan nikale.

Meaning: Invent.

### Memory Trick

**Devise = Design**

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning |
|--------|--------|
| Device | Tool |
| Devise | Create |

# 401. Ensure / Insure / Assure

## Type
Three-Word Confusion

---

## Word 1: Ensure

### Simple English Meaning
To make certain that something happens.

### Roman Nepali Meaning
Nischit garnu

### Examples

English: Please ensure the door is locked.

Roman Nepali: Dhoka banda cha bhanera nischit gara.

Meaning: Make certain.

English: We must ensure safety.

Roman Nepali: Hami le suraksha nischit garnu parchha.

Meaning: Guarantee.

### Memory Trick

**Ensure = Make sure**

---

## Word 2: Insure

### Simple English Meaning
To protect financially through insurance.

### Roman Nepali Meaning
Bima garnu

### Examples

English: I insured my car.

Roman Nepali: Maile aafno gaadiko bima gare.

Meaning: Covered by insurance.

English: Many people insure their homes.

Roman Nepali: Dherai manchhele ghar ko bima garchhan.

Meaning: Financial protection.

### Memory Trick

**Insure = Insurance**

---

## Word 3: Assure

### Simple English Meaning
To tell someone confidently that something is true.

### Roman Nepali Meaning
Dhukka parnu

### Examples

English: I assure you everything is fine.

Roman Nepali: Ma timilai dhukka parchu sabai thik cha.

Meaning: Give confidence.

English: She assured her friend.

Roman Nepali: Unile saathilai dhukka parin.

Meaning: Reassure.

### Memory Trick

**Assure = Confidence**

---

## Comparison Summary

| Word | Meaning |
|--------|--------|
| Ensure | Make certain |
| Insure | Provide insurance |
| Assure | Reassure someone |
